# Intimidation Artifact
Categories: Spooky Festival, Rare items, Accessories, Crafting ingredients, Events, Artifact
Spooky Festival Exclusive Intimidation Artifact is only obtainable during the [[Spooky Festival]].
[Intimidation Artifact]
Type: Accessory ([[Accessories]])
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Upgrades]
← Previous: [[Intimidation Ring]]
Next →: [[Intimidation Relic]]
[Item Properties]
Upgradeable: ✕ No
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Fear Mongerer]]
Buy: 100x (1 stack plus 36 (100 in total)) [[Purple Candy]] 1x [[Intimidation Ring]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 10,000 [[Coins]] 100x [[Green Candy]] 100x [[Purple Candy]]
Raw Materials to upgrade: 100x [[Purple Candy]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: INTIMIDATION_ARTIFACT
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +8; Monsters at or below Level 25 will no longer target you.; Works while in Accessory Bag!; RARE ACCESSORY
The Intimidation Artifact is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] and an upgraded form of the [[Intimidation Ring]]. It prevents mobs at or below level 25 from attacking the player.
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
The Intimidation Artifact can be purchased from the [[Fear Mongerer]] for 100 (1 stack plus 36) [[Purple Candy]] and an 1 [[Intimidation Ring]] during the [[Spooky Festival]].
## Usage
Accessory:
Intimidation Artifact
[Effects]
[Monsters at or below level 25 will no longer target you.]
While the Intimidation Artifact prevents most mobs at or below level 25 from attacking the player, some mobs are not affected. While not specified by the game, it is likely this behavior is intended.
These mobs include:
- [Lv15] [[Minos Hunter]]
- All [[Sea Creatures]]
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Intimidation Relic]]|None|[[Intimidation Artifact]] 1x Intimidation Artifact 8x [[Scare Fragment]]|Recipe: [[Scare Fragment]] ×8 + Intimidation Artifact → [[Intimidation Relic]]|
## Trivia
- When paired with the [[Parrot Pet]]'s Flamboyant ability, this accessory can stop Level 45 mobs from attacking you.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Oct 24, 2019|0.7.3|Added Intimidation Artifact.|
|Dec 8, 2022|0.17.1|Fixed some mobs not being affected by the Intimidation Artifact.|
|Oct 24, 2023|0.19.6|Changed Intimidation Artifact texture.|
|Oct 24, 2023|0.19.6|The Intimidation Artifact can be upgraded to the [[Intimidation Relic]].|

# Party Chest
Aliases: Party Chests
Categories: Spooky Festival, Events
Spooky Festival Exclusive Party Chest is only obtainable during the [[Spooky Festival]].
[Party Chest]
The Party Chest is a chest that has a 1-3% chance (depending on the Mob) of appearing when killing any mob during the [[Spooky Festival]] whilst [[Foxy|Foxy's]] A Time for Giving perk is active. After the player interacts with a Party Chest, it will open similarly to a [[Dungeon Reward Chest]]. If the chest is left untouched it will despawn and disappear after some time. The chest will also despawn if the player leaves a 20-block radius surrounding the Party Chest.
The player cannot open another player's Party Chest or steal their rewards.
## Rewards
Opening a Party Chest rewards the player with 2-4 pieces of loot.

|[[Ectoplasm]]|[[Ectoplasm]]|2-4|55|11.387% (1 in 8.78 rolls)|1.025|
|[[Blast o' Lantern]]|[[Blast o' Lantern]]|2-4|35|7.246% (1 in 13.8 rolls)|0.652|
|[[Pumpkin Guts]]|[[Pumpkin Guts]]|1-2|20|4.141% (1 in 24.15 rolls)|0.186|
|[[Candy Corn]]|[[Candy Corn]]|1|15|3.106% (1 in 32.2 rolls)|0.093|

|[[Bat Person Talisman]]|[[Bat Person Talisman]]|1|5|1.035% (1 in 96.6 rolls)|0.031|
|[[Party Necklace]]|[[Party Necklace]]|1|5|1.035% (1 in 96.6 rolls)|0.031|
|[[Party Belt]]|[[Party Belt]]|1|5|1.035% (1 in 96.6 rolls)|0.031|

## Bugs
- Despite the patch notes stating that Party Chests can also appear the hour before and after the [[Spooky Festival]], they only appear during the actual event.

# Pumpkin Guts
Categories: Spooky Festival, Uncommon items, Items in Sacks, Items, Crafting ingredients, Events
Spooky Festival Exclusive Pumpkin Guts is only obtainable during the [[Spooky Festival]].
[Pumpkin Guts]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Source: [[Trick or Treat Chest]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: [[Spooky Sack]]; [[Events Sack]]
[Shop]
Sell: 10,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PUMPKIN_GUTS
Tooltip: Why does Halloween have to be so gross?; Right-click to view recipes!; UNCOMMON
Pumpkin Guts are an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item that can be obtained only from [[Trick or Treat Chest|Trick or Treat Chests]]. It is used in crafting the [[Spooky Sack]], [[Pumpkin Launcher]] and [[Blast o' Lantern|Blast o' Lanterns]].
## Obtaining
Pumpkin Guts have a 4.16% chance to drop from a TREAT [[Trick or Treat Chest]]. Seeing as Trick or Treat Chests have a 75% chance to be the TREAT variation, this means that the overall chance to drop Pumpkin Guts from any [[Trick or Treat Chest]] is 3.125%.
Alternatively, Pumpkin Guts have a 4.14% chance to drop from a [[Party Chest]] in quantities of 1-2.
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Spooky Sack]]|None|1x [[Trick or Treat Bag]] [[Pumpkin Guts]] 32x Pumpkin Guts|Recipe: Pumpkin Guts x4 ×8 + [[Trick or Treat Bag]] → [[Spooky Sack]]|
|[[Blast o' Lantern]]|None|[[Pumpkin Guts]] 1x Pumpkin Guts 8x [[Green Candy]]|Recipe: [[Green Candy]] ×8 + Pumpkin Guts → [[Blast o' Lantern]] x32|
|[[Pumpkin Launcher]]|None|1x [[Blast o' Lantern]] [[Pumpkin Guts]] 24x Pumpkin Guts 40x [[Ectoplasm]]|Recipe: Pumpkin Guts x4 ×6 + [[Ectoplasm]] x20 ×2 + [[Blast o' Lantern]] → [[Pumpkin Launcher]]|
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Oct 30, 2020|0.9.9|Added Pumpkin Guts.|

# Purple Candy
Aliases: Purple Candies
Categories: Spooky Festival, Epic items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mob Drops, Crafting ingredients, Events, Candy
Spooky Festival Exclusive Purple Candy is only obtainable during the [[Spooky Festival]].
[Purple Candy]
Type: Item
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Material Tiers]
← Previous: [[Green Candy]]
Next →: None
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: [[Spooky Sack]]; [[Events Sack]]
[Shop]
Sell: 5,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PURPLE_CANDY
Tooltip: Candy can be used to trade with the Fear Mongerer during the Spooky Festival!; EPIC
Purple Candy is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item obtained mainly by killing [[Mob|Mobs]] during the [[Spooky Festival]], among other related sources.
## Obtaining
### Spooky Festival
While the [[Spooky Festival]] is active, killing [[Mob|Mobs]] has a 10% chance to grant [[Green Candy]]. This can be increased up to nearly 100% with [[Green Candy|various modifiers, shown here]].
When obtained, there is a base 10% chance of it being a Purple Candy instead. This conversion chance is increased with the following bonuses, which is also not affected by [[Magic Find]]:
Increasing Purple Candy Chance:
|Slot|Name|Extra Chance|Notes|
|-|-|-|-|
||[[Mob]] Level|+0.2% per level|For example, a [Lv10] mob has a +2% conversion chance.|
|Purple Vogue I|Purple Vogue ([[Doug]]) Perk|+1% → +3%|Chance increases with a higher perk level, up to level 3.|
|[[Spooky Armor]]|[[Spooky Armor]]|5%|From the Full Set Bonus|
|[[Bat Person Armor]]|[[Bat Person Armor]]|5%|From the Full Set Bonus|
For example, a [[Ghost]], which is [Lv250], grants an additional +50% chance. When combined with the base 10% conversion chance and the upgrades listed above, this results in a 68% conversion chance.
Without any bonuses to [[Green Candy]] or Purple Candy chances, that means [[Green Candy]] has a 10% obtaining chance, and [[Purple Candy]] Purple Candy has a 1% obtaining chance.

Helping kill a [[Headless Horseman]] can also grant Purple Candy.
### Bat Firework
Using a [[Bat Firework]] will grant the player between 5-64 Purple Candy, with an average of about 11 per use.
|Purple Candy; Amount|Weight|Chance|
|-|-|-|
|5|40|34.483%|
|10|40|34.483%|
|15|20|17.241%|
|20|10|8.621%|
|25|5|4.310%|
|64|1|0.862%|
### Minions
Purple Candy has a 0.015% chance to be obtained per item generated from [[Minions]] with the [[Sleepy Hollow]] upgrade applied.
### Trick or Treat Chests
Opening a [[Trick or Treat Chest]] has a 17.71% chance to grant 1-4 Purple Candy.
### Party Chests
Opening a [[Party Chest]] has a 18.012% chance to grant 2-6 Purple Candy.
### Great Spook Tree
A [[Great Spook Tree]] can be placed on the player's [[Private Island]] to occasionally generate Purple Candy.
### Garden Visitors
Accepting the [[Fear Mongerer]]'s [[Garden Visitors|Garden Visitor]] request will grant a [[Green Candy]] or Purple Candy.
## Usage
Purple Candy can be traded the [[Fear Mongerer]] while they are in the [[Village]] in exchange for spooky-themed items, as is the case with [[Green Candy]].
Obtaining Purple Candy awards the player +5 points in the [[Spooky Festival]], while [[Green Candy]] only gives 1 point. This is only the case for candies dropped directly from killing mobs or by collecting candies from Bats (excluding players' Bat Fireworks).

## Trivia
- [[Magic Find]] does not affect the chances of dropping a Purple Candy.

# Rock Candy
Aliases: Sweet
Categories: Spooky Festival, Uncommon items, Power stones, Power Stones
Spooky Festival Exclusive Rock Candy is only obtainable during the [[Spooky Festival]].
Accessories have never tasted so sweet.
[Rock Candy]
Type: Accessory Power Stone
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: Accessories have never tasted so sweet.
[Reforge/Power Stone]
Power: Sweet
Req. Skill Level: Combat XV (15) ([[Combat]])
[Item Properties]

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ROCK_CANDY
Tooltip: Power Stone Combine x9 of this item at the Thaumaturgist in the Hub to unlock the Sweet power.; Accessories have never tasted so sweet.; At 1,000 Magical Power:; +488.56&#xE010; Health +348.97&#xE008; Defense +38.77&#xE022; Speed; Unique Power Bonus:; +5&#xE022; Speed; ❣ Requires Combat Skill 15.; UNCOMMON POWER STONE
Rock Candy is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Power Stones|Power Stone]], which unlocks the [[Power Stones|Sweet]] power for [[Accessories]], granting [[Health]], [[Defense]], and [[Speed]]. It also provides +5 [[Speed]] as its unique power bonus.
## Obtaining
Rock Candy can be obtained during the [[Spooky Festival]] as a 2.083% chance drop from [[Trick or Treat Chest|Trick or Treat Chests]].
## Requirements
Rock Candy has a [[Combat|Combat XV (15)]] requirement and is only used as a [[Power Stones|Power Stone]] for [[Accessories]]. Combining 9 Rock Candy at [[Maxwell]] will unlock the Sweet power permanently.

## Trivia
- This item was created by xTear (with reforge stats by Rainbowcraft2), and xTear was awarded the 10th [[Creative Mind]] from xHascox.
- The original description of the item was, "Everyone has a sweet tooth, and it is never stronger than the candy festival of the year." However, before it was added to the game, the description was changed to what it is now.
- The original rarity was suggested to be [[Rarity|EPIC]], but upon release, it was [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]]. It was later changed to [[Rarity|RARE]], and then eventually changed back to [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]].

## Trivia
- The Skeleton Horse Pet had a bug that allowed players to trample crops on other [[Private Island|Private Islands]] while guesting.
- The Skeleton Horse Pet was the first pet added into the game, added via update 0.7.3.
  - The [[Snowman Pet]] was the second pet in the game, added via update 0.7.5. The other pets were added during 0.7.6, 0.7.8, and 0.8.1
- It used to be possible for players who abused bugs with the Skeleton Horse Pet to have their pet removed and be prevented from ever earning it again on that profile. Screenshot of the pet being removed
  - These restrictions were lifted at some point with the new pet system.
## Gallery
- The Skeleton Horse Pet's former appearance when summoned.

## Usage
Right-clicking a [[Pets|Pet]] whilst holding a Spooky Cupcake will equip it to the [[Pets|Pet]], granting it +20 [[Speed]] and +30 [[Strength]]. If another [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] were equipped on the [[Pets|Pet]], it would refund the Spooky Cupcake for 50,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.
## Obtaining
Spooky Cupcake has a 1.67% to drop from a [[Trick or Treat Chest]] whilst the [[Spooky Festival]] is active. Only 1 can drop at a time.

# Spooky Fragment
Aliases: Spooky Shard
Categories: Spooky Festival, Epic items, Items in Sacks, Items, Crafting ingredients, Events, Mob Drops
Spooky Festival Exclusive Spooky Fragment is only obtainable during the [[Spooky Festival]].
[Spooky Fragment]
Type: Item
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Source: [[Headless Horseman]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: [[Spooky Sack]]; [[Events Sack]]
[Shop]
Sell: 10,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SPOOKY_SHARD
Tooltip: Right-click to view recipes!; EPIC
A Spooky Fragment is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item dropped by the [[Headless Horseman]]. It is used to craft certain items from the [[Spooky Festival]].
## Obtaining
Spooky Fragments are dropped by the [[Headless Horseman]]. If a [[Headless Horseman]] is killed, the player who spawned it receives [[Spooky Fragment]] 4 Spooky Fragments, the first place damager receives [[Spooky Fragment]] 1 Spooky Fragment, and the second place damager receives [[Spooky Fragment]] 1 Spooky Fragment if they dealt over half of the [[Damage]] dealt by the first place damager.
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Bat Person Helmet ([[Bat Person Armor]])|None|1x Spooky Helmet ([[Spooky Armor]]) [[Spooky Fragment]] 5x Spooky Fragment|Recipe: Spooky Fragment ×5 + [[Spooky Armor]] → [[Bat Person Armor]]|
|Bat Person Chestplate ([[Bat Person Armor]])|None|1x Spooky Chestplate ([[Spooky Armor]]) [[Spooky Fragment]] 8x Spooky Fragment|Recipe: Spooky Fragment ×8 + [[Spooky Armor]] → [[Bat Person Armor]]|
|Bat Person Leggings ([[Bat Person Armor]])|None|1x Spooky Leggings ([[Spooky Armor]]) [[Spooky Fragment]] 7x Spooky Fragment|Recipe: Spooky Fragment ×7 + [[Spooky Armor]] → [[Bat Person Armor]]|
|Bat Person Boots ([[Bat Person Armor]])|None|1x Spooky Boots ([[Spooky Armor]]) [[Spooky Fragment]] 4x Spooky Fragment|Recipe: Spooky Fragment ×4 + [[Spooky Armor]] → [[Bat Person Armor]]|
|[[Ghoul Buster]]|None|[[Spooky Fragment]] 8x Spooky Fragment 32x [[Ectoplasm]] 64x [[Purple Candy]]|Recipe: [[Ectoplasm]] x16 ×2 + Spooky Fragment x4 ×2 + [[Purple Candy]] x64 → [[Ghoul Buster]]|
|[[Bat Person Ring]]|None|1x [[Bat Person Talisman]] [[Spooky Fragment]] 4x Spooky Fragment 32x [[Ectoplasm]]|Recipe: Spooky Fragment ×4 + [[Ectoplasm]] x8 ×4 + [[Bat Person Talisman]] → [[Bat Person Ring]]|
|[[Bat Person Artifact]]|None|1x [[Bat Person Ring]] [[Spooky Fragment]] 16x Spooky Fragment 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Ectoplasm]]|Recipe: Spooky Fragment x4 ×4 + [[Ectoplasm]] x32 ×4 + [[Bat Person Ring]] → [[Bat Person Artifact]]|

# Spooky Pie
Categories: Spooky Festival, Special items, Items, Events
Spooky Festival Exclusive Spooky Pie is only obtainable during the [[Spooky Festival]].
[Spooky Pie]
Type: Special item
Rarity: SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: Obtained during the [number] nth Spooky Festival during SkyBlock Year [Year].
Source: [[Spooky Festival]]
Obtained: By placing in the top 10,000 in the [[Spooky Festival]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No; (Except to Co-op members)
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Co-op)
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SPOOKY_PIE
Tooltip: Obtained during the [number] Spooky Festival during SkyBlock Year [year].; Player: [player]; Position: [position]; Candy Score: [score]; * Co-op Soulbound * SPECIAL
Spooky Pie is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] item reward from the [[Spooky Festival]], given to players who end up placing in the Top 10,000 in the event.
Each event cycle, the pie awarded is numbered for how many SkyBlock years have occurred (a year is 5 days 4 hours). For example, a Spooky Pie for the "1st Spooky Festival" will never be obtainable from the event again.

# Trick or Treat Bag
Aliases: Treat Bag
Categories: Spooky Festival, Uncommon items, Items, Pages with non-auctionable items with valid auctionable item IDs, Crafting ingredients, Events, Container
Spooky Festival Exclusive Trick or Treat Bag is only obtainable during the [[Spooky Festival]].
[Trick or Treat Bag]
Type: Backpack item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: Picking up candy will place it directly into this bag! Your Candy inventory is shared across your profile - opening any Trick or Treat Bag will display your Candy inventory.
Source: [[Fear Mongerer]] stock
[Upgrades]
← Previous: None
Next →: [[Spooky Sack]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes (empty)
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Fear Mongerer]]
Buy: 1 [[Green Candy]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: TRICK_OR_TREAT_BAG
Tooltip: Picking up Candy will place it directly into this bag! Your Candy inventory is shared across your profile - opening any Trick or Treat Bag will display your Candy inventory.; UNCOMMON
The Trick or Treat Bag is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] backpack item that has 27 slots for [[Candy (disambiguation)|Candies]]. It also automatically picks up and stores your candies inside the bag.
It can be upgraded into the [[Spooky Sack]].
## Obtaining
The Trick or Treat Bag can be purchased from the [[Fear Mongerer]] during the [[Spooky Festival]].
### Purchase
|Merchant|Item|
|-|-|
|[[Fear Mongerer]]|1 [[Green Candy]]|
## Usage
Trick or Treat Bag is mainly used to store large amounts of candy without taking up inventory space.
As a bag, it cannot be put into the player's [[Ender Chest]] or [[Backpack|Backpacks]], though it can be put into [[Private Island]] chests or the [[Personal Vault]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Spooky Sack]]|None|[[Trick or Treat Bag]] 1x Trick or Treat Bag 32x [[Pumpkin Guts]]|Recipe: [[Pumpkin Guts]] x4 ×8 + Trick or Treat Bag → [[Spooky Sack]]|
## Trivia
- The Trick or Treat Bag inventory is tied to the player's profile, meaning having multiple of them does not allow the player to store more candy.

|Jun 1, 2021|The Trick or Treat Bag has been re-enabled.|
|Oct 15, 2026|Fixed being unable to pick up Candy with a full Trick or Treat Bag.|

# Trick or Treat Chest
Aliases: Spooky Chest, Trick or Treat Chests
Categories: Spooky Festival, Events
Spooky Festival Exclusive Trick or Treat Chest is only obtainable during the [[Spooky Festival]].
[Trick or Treat Chest]
The Trick or Treat Chest is a chest that has a 1-3% chance (depending on the Mob) of appearing when killing any mob during the [[Spooky Festival]]. After the player interacts with it, it will open similarly to a [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Chest]] and either reward the player with TREAT or spawn a [[Spooky|Spooky Mob]] with TRICK. If the chest is left untouched, it will despawn and disappear after some time.
The player cannot open other players' Trick or Treat Chests or steal their rewards.
## Outcomes
The TREAT outcome can give 1-3 types of loot. Opening a Trick or Treat Chest has a 75% chance to result in a TREAT.
The TRICK outcome will spawn a [[Spooky|Spooky Mob]]. Opening a Trick or Treat Chest has a 25% chance to result in a TRICK.
TREAT:
|Slot|Item|Amount|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Green Candy]]|[[Green Candy]]|4-8|26.04% (1/3.4)|
|[[Purple Candy]]|[[Purple Candy]]|1-4|17.71% (17/96)|
|[[Ectoplasm]]|[[Ectoplasm]]|1-4|11% (11/96)|
|[[Pumpkin Guts]]|[[Pumpkin Guts]]|1|4.17% (1/24)|
|[[Blast o' Lantern]]|[[Blast o' Lantern]]|1-2|7.29% (7/96)|
|[[Rock Candy]]|[[Rock Candy]]|1|2.08% (1/48)|
|[[Candy Corn]]|[[Candy Corn]]|1|3.13% (1/32)|
|[[Vampirism]]|Vampirism VI ([[Vampirism]])|1|0.21% (1/480)|
|[[Spooky Cupcake]]|[[Spooky Cupcake]]|1|1.67% (1/60)|
|[[Bat Person Talisman]]|[[Bat Person Talisman]]|1|1.04% (1/96)|
|[[Candy the Fish]]|[[Candy the Fish]]|1|0.21% (1/480)|
TRICK:
|Mob|Chance|
|-|-|
|[[Crazy Witch]]|6.12% (3/49)|
|[[Wraith]]|5.1% (1/19.6)|
|[[Wither Gourd]]|4.08% (1/24.5)|
|[[Phantom Spirit]]|3.57% (1/28)|
|[[Trick or Treater]]|3.06% (3/98)|
|[[Scary Jerry]]|3.06% (3/98)|
## Trivia
- Trick or Treat Chests have Trick or Treat? above them.
## Gallery
- A Trick or Treat Chest in the world
## History

|Oct 30, 2020|0.9.9|Added Trick or Treat Chests.|

# Vampire Mask
Categories: Spooky Festival, Rare items, Armor, Crafting ingredients, Events, Helmet
Spooky Festival Exclusive Vampire Mask is only obtainable during the [[Spooky Festival]].
[Vampire Mask]
Type: [[Armor]]
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Obtained: Party Chests ([[Party Chest]])
Stats: [[Defense]]: 111
[Upgrades]
← Previous: None
Next →: [[Vampire Witch Mask]]
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✔ Yes
Reforgeable: ✔ Yes
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes (+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]))
[Shop]
Sell: 10 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: VAMPIRE_MASK
Tooltip: Defense: +111; Ability: Rejuvenate While wearing, drains 5 health per second from allmonsters within 8; blocks.; This item can be reforged!; RARE HELMET
A Vampire Mask is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Helmets|Helmet]] obtained from [[Party Chest|Party Chests]] while [[Foxy]] is in office with the A Time for Giving perk active.
## Obtaining
A Vampire Mask can be obtained from [[Party Chest|Party Chests]] during the [[Spooky Festival]] while [[Foxy]] is in office with the A Time for Giving perk active.
## Usage
Helmet:
Vampire Mask [[Defense]]: +111
[Effects]
[Ability: Rejuvenate; While wearing, drains 5 health per second from all monsters within 8 blocks.]
While the Vampire Mask is worn, all monsters within 8 blocks are damaged for 5 [[Health]] every second. The wearer heals for the amount the ability damages mobs. The ability does not trigger [[Thunderlord]] or [[Thunderbolt]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Vampire Witch Mask]]|None|1x [[Witch Mask]] [[Vampire Mask]] 1x Vampire Mask 7x [[Ectoplasm]]|Recipe: [[Ectoplasm]] ×7 + [[Witch Mask]] + Vampire Mask → [[Vampire Witch Mask]]|
## Trivia
- The Vampire Mask's ability triggers an additional hit on [[Zealot|Zealots]], thus granting the 10% bonus Special Zealot chance.
- The Vampire Mask cannot be used on the [[Private Island]].
- The Vampire Mask used to be obtainable by placing in the top 10 or by getting 2,000 points or above in a [[Spooky Festival]] before October 30, 2020 before being replaced with the [[Witch Mask]].
  - It was still possible to receive the Vampire Mask from the [[Spooky Festival]] after its replacement, but only during the bonus event from Mayor Foxy's perk, as it used the old system and rewards.
## History

|Jul 2, 2024|0.20.3|Vampire Mask can now be obtained from Party Chests while [[Foxy]] is in office with 'A Time for Giving' perk.|

Your bats will not spawn on your island.; Ability: Rejuvenate While wearing, drains 5; health per second from all monsters within 8 blocks.; This item can be reforged!; EPIC HELMET
The Vampire Witch Mask is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Helmets|Helmet]]. It's a fusion of the [[Vampire Mask]] and [[Witch Mask]], granting both stats and bonuses. All [[Enchantments]] and [[Reforging|Reforges]] on the [[Vampire Mask]] are lost upon crafting, but those on the [[Witch Mask]] are transferred.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Witch Mask]] 1x [[Vampire Mask]] 7x [[Ectoplasm]]|Recipe: [[Ectoplasm]] ×7 + [[Witch Mask]] + [[Vampire Mask]] → Vampire Witch Mask|
## Usage
Helmet:
Vampire Witch Mask [[Health]]: +132 [[Defense]]: +222
[Effects]
[Ability: Bat Swarm; Summons two bats that that will follow you and attack nearby enemies, dealing 3,000 damage upon exploding. Your bats will not spawn on your island.; Intelligence Scaling (Damage Calculation): 0.2 Ability: Rejuvenate; While wearing, drain 5 health per second from all monsters within 8 blocks.]
## Tips
- Equipping the Vampire Witch Mask with a [[Bat Person Talisman]] (or higher) will grant a small bonus to [[Defense]], [[Strength]], and [[Intelligence]].
- If a summoned bat kills a mob without the player's contribution, the kill will not be counted, nor will there be any combat xp.
  - [[Scavenger]] only applies when the mob killed by the helmet's abilities has received damage from the player.
## Trivia
- The Vampire Witch Mask's appearance is the helmet of the Witch King of Angmar from the Lord of the Rings.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Oct 30, 2020|0.9.9|Added Vampire Witch Mask.|

## Trivia
- Regenerative Howl does not heal the wearer, only players around them.
- Werewolf Armor was the first [[Armor]] in the game to directly grant [[Ferocity]].
- Excluding Infernal Armor Sets, Werewolf Armor has the highest amount of essence required to be dungeonized and starred.
  - This is followed by the [[Wither Armor]] variants and then [[Holy Dragon Armor]].
## Tips
- Due to the lack of any [[Combat]] requirement, Werewolf Armor is an alternative to [[Unstable Dragon Armor]] and [[Strong Dragon Armor]] in the early game.
- It also offers a similar DPS to [[Shadow Assassin Armor]] due to its 40 [[Ferocity]] and [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] rarity for reforges.

ID: WITHER_GOURD_40
Wither Gourd is a [[Spooky|Spooky Mob]] that spawns only during [[Spooky Festival|Spooky Festivals]] at night.
## Spawning
Wither Gourds spawn in the [[Graveyard]], [[Wilderness]], [[Spider's Den]] and [[Obsidian Sanctuary]] at night during [[Spooky Festival|Spooky Festivals]]. They have a 4.08% chance of being spawned from a [[Trick or Treat Chest]].
## Behavior
Wither Gourds have 11 [[Health]] and deal 50 [[Damage]]. They attack the player with [[Blast o' Lantern|Blast o' Lanterns]], dealing [[True Damage]] and [[Knockback|True Knockback]]. Like other [[Spooky|Spooky Mobs]], it takes only 1 [[Damage]] every time it is hit.

# Slayer Weapons
Categories: Swords, Slayer, Bows, Weapons, Melee Weapons
[Slayer Weapons]
A Slayer Weapon is a weapon tied to a [[Slayer]], either unlocked through that Slayer's leveling rewards or built to deal increased damage against its mob type. Slayer Weapons are the main way to progress a Slayer track, because the majority of them multiply damage against the specific mobs a quest requires. Every Slayer has its own weapon line that grows in power as the player levels it.
## Overview
Most Slayer Weapons share common abilities against the Slayer's corresponding [[Mob Types|mob type]]:
- Grants an additive [[Damage]] multiplier.
- Grants [[Combat Wisdom]], increasing [[Combat|Combat XP]] from that mob type.
- Reduce damage taken from the mob type.
Some also carry a utility ability like clearing the [[Tarantula Broodfather]]'s [[Web|Cobwebs]] or vanquishing the [[Riftstalker Bloodfiend]] below 20% [[Health]] .
Due to the fact that the bonus is tied to a specific mob type, a Slayer Weapon is usually far stronger than a generic sword of the same rarity inside its own Slayer, and much weaker outside it.
A few weapons from Slayers sit outside the main damage line. These are sidegrades, utility tools, or weapons that share the same source but serve a different purpose, such as the [[Reaper Scythe]], [[Sinseeker Scythe]], [[Edible Mace]], or [[Steak Stake]].
The Zombie Slayer line targets [[Undead (Mob Type)|Undead]] mobs, starting with the Revenant Falchion at level III and ending with the Halberd of the Shredded at level VIII.

## Progression
Each Slayer line upgrades into the next tier, keeping the same bonus type while raising its value.
Slayer: Weapon line
Zombie ([[Zombie Slayer]]): [[Revenant Falchion]] → [[Reaper Falchion]] → [[Halberd of the Shredded]]
Spider ([[Spider Slayer]]): [[Recluse Fang]] → [[Tarantula Fang]] → [[Scorpion Foil]] → [[Sting]]
Wolf ([[Wolf Slayer]]): [[Shaman Sword]] → [[Pooch Sword]]
Enderman ([[Enderman Slayer]]): [[Voidwalker Katana]] → [[Voidedge Katana]] → [[Vorpal Katana]] → [[Atomsplit Katana]]
Blaze ([[Blaze Slayer]]): [[Firedust Dagger]] / [[Twilight Dagger]] → [[Kindlebane Dagger]] / [[Mawdredge Dagger]] → [[Pyrochaos Dagger]] / [[Deathripper Dagger]]
Vampire ([[Vampire Slayer]]): [[Silver-Laced Karambit]] → [[Silver-Twist Karambit]]
## Slayer Weapon Buffs
Two mechanics explicitly increase the effectiveness of Slayer Weapons.
|Name|Type|Effect|
|-|-|-|
|[[Habanero Tactics]]|Ultimate Enchantment (Armor)|Deal +20-25% damage with Slayer Weapons, gain +2.5-5 [[Combat Wisdom]] with Slayer Weapons, and heal +6-7.5% more from wands.; Requires Enchanting XXVIII (28) ([[Enchanting]]) to apply.|
|Smoldering Polarization ([[Re-heated Gummy Polar Bear]])|Consumable effect (1 hour)|While active with [[Habanero Tactics]], Slayer Weapons gain +10-15 [[Magic Find]] against the mob they are made to kill.|
## Trivia
- Every Slayer has at least one weapon that grants bonus damage against its respective mob type.
- The [[Terminator]] is the only Slayer Weapon that reduces a player stat, dividing [[Crit Chance]] by 4.
- The Blaze Slayer line is the only one made of paired weapons, with one Dagger for each Hellion Shield attunement.
- The Vampire Slayer line uses [[Rift Damage]] instead of regular melee stats, so its weapons only function inside the [[Rift Dimension|Rift]].
Linked from: [[Combat Wisdom]], [[Consumables]], [[Slayer (disambiguation)]]

# Weapons
Aliases: Weapon
Categories: Swords, Bows, Wands, Weapons
[Weapons]
Weapons are items that consist of Swords, Bows, Longswords, Shortbows, and Gauntlets. They are used primarily to deal damage to [[Mob|mobs]], but some weapons serve as support items rather than other primary sources of damage.
## Types of Weapons
### Swords
[[Swords]] are short-ranged melee weapons that deal damage to mobs within the player's [[Swing Range]]. They are designed for close-range combat.
[[Longswords]] are variants of Swords that grant increased [[Swing Range]], allowing the player to hit mobs from further away.
### Bows
[[Bows]] are ranged weapons that deal damage through projectiles: the distance a Bow's projectile travels is dependent on how long the Right-Click button is held for prior to releasing. They are designed for long-range combat.
[[Shortbows]] are variants of Bows that do not have a draw time: pressing the Right-Click button instantly fires a projectile, as does Left-Click.
### Wands
[[Wands]] are items that consume [[Vitality]] to cast abilities. They usually serve as support items and cannot be modified with [[Enchantments]] and [[Reforging|Reforges]], with the exception of [[Ultimate Wise]].
### Gauntlets
[[Gemstone Gauntlet|Gauntlets]] are items that function both as [[Pickaxes]] and [[Swords]], and as such can have both modifiers exclusive to Pickaxes and Swords applied to it. Currently, the only Gauntlet in the game is the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]].
## Trivia
- Many weapons in SkyBlock are references to other minigames on the Hypixel Network or other video games, such as League of Legends.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Weapons.|

# Armor & Equipment
Aliases: Fishing Gear, Fishing gear, Gear
Categories: Temp/Using Skill Aliases, Armor, Equipment
[Armor & Equipment]
Category: Mechanics
## Overview
- Pieces of Armor & Equipment can be:
  - [[Armor]] : [[Helmets]], [[Chestplates]], [[Leggings]], [[Boots]], accessed from /wardrobe or /wd or simply from the [[Player|Inventory]] by pressing E.
  - [[Equipment]] : [[Necklaces]], [[Cloaks]], [[Belts]], [[Gloves]], accessed from /equipment or /eq.
- For both of these, synonyms may be used (e.g. [[Mangrove Grippers]] and [[Dwarven Handwarmers]] and [[Gauntlet of Contagion]] can be equipped in the Gloves slot.
- The player has 8 slots unlocked, one for each [[Armor]] and [[Equipment]] type of piece, all unlocked by default.
- Armor & Equipment pieces are only active when equipped in their respective slot.
- The [[Wardrobe]] and the [[Equipment Wardrobe]] can be used to store and freely swap between Armor Sets in a click.
- The [[Loadouts]] can be used to freely swap between Armor Sets, Equipment Sets, Power Stones, Tuning Points – among others – in a click, accessed from /loadouts.

## Exceptions
- In the [[Rift Dimension]]:
  - The player has an entirely separate inventory including Armor & Equipment that is saved between visits.
  - Some items may bear the following properties allowing them to be transferred and or used both in and out of it:
    - [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]]: Present in both dimensions. [[Rift Dimension]] ↔ [[Hub Island|Overworld]].
    - [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Exportable #]]: Only present in the [[Rift Dimension]]. [[Rift Dimension]] → [[Hub Island|Overworld]].
    - [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Exported #]]: Only present in the [[Hub Island|Overworld]] for an item that used to be [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Exportable #]] from the [[Rift Dimension]].
- In the [[Critter Safari]]:
  - The player has an entirely separate inventory including Armor & Equipment that is not saved between visits except for the [[Sparkling Amulet]] and the [[Safari Belt]].
  - The player cannot un-equip Armor & Equipment.
  - If the player has reforged their [[Safari Belt]] outside of the [[Critter Safari]], then its [[Critter Safari]] counterpart will always bear that reforge onward.
- In the [[Dojo]]:
  - In order to enter a test, [[Master Tao]] requires the player to have an empty inventory and Armor & Equipment slots, aside from the [[SkyBlock Menu]].
## Bugs
- Armor Swapping has been - and still is – notoriously used in Exploits and abuses (e.g. Duplication, decreasing the performance in a lobby).
## Trivia
- "Armor Swapping" or "Equipment Swapping" refer to the quick use of the [[Wardrobe]], [[Equipment Wardrobe]] and or [[Loadouts]] systems by players to repeatedly change their Armor & Equipment in order to optimize an action. This is mostly done through 3rd-party modifications binding a key to a command (e.g. pressing = runs the /loadouts command).
  - One of the common applications is found in [[Pests|Pest]] Farming where the player uses a high [[Bonus Pest Chance]] loadout while breaking crops and a high [[Farming Fortune]] for killing [[Pests]] on [[The Garden]].
Linked from: [[Armor]], [[Boots]], [[Chestplates]], [[Equipment]], [[Helmets]]

# Catacombs
Aliases: Cata, Catacomb, Master Mode, Mastermode, The Catacombs, The Catacombs - Floor, The Catacombs - Master Mode
Categories: Temp/Using Skill Aliases, Confirmations needed, More info needed, Dungeons, Catacombs
[Catacombs]
The Catacombs are the first [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] in SkyBlock. It can be accessed through speaking to [[Mort]] in the [[Catacombs Entrance]] or [[Dungeon Hub]].
Players are, by default, required to be in a party of 2-5 [[Player|players]] in order to join the Catacombs. [[Bingo|Ⓑ Bingo]] profiles are exempt from the normal party requirement. Giving [[Mort]] a [[Saul's Recommendation]] from [[Adventurer Saul]] also allows players to enter the Catacombs on their own. You can set up your own party and invite your friends to join, or you can set up a party through the Party Finder, where you can select different options and requirements for people to join your party.
## Overview
- The Catacombs are a series of floors, each with interconnecting [[Catacombs Rooms|rooms]] filled with [[Catacombs Mobs|mobs]], [[Dungeon Secrets|secrets]], [[Dungeon Traps|traps]], and [[Catacombs Puzzle Rooms|puzzles]].
- The main goal of a run is to progress through the floor, defeat [[The Watcher]], and then defeat the [[Bosses|BOSS]].
- After killing all mobs in a room that have a ✯ star in their nametag, the room is marked as cleared and will drop:
  - a [[Dungeon Blessing|Blessing]] and/or a [[Superboom TNT]]
  - a [[Wither Key]], which unlocks more sections of the floor. The [[Blood Key]] is dropped when killing all ✯ mobs in the last room before the [[The Watcher|Blood Room]] and will unlock said room, where [[The Watcher]] summons [[The Watcher|Undeads]] that must be killed in order to progress to the [[Bosses|BOSS]] room.
### Rules
While in the Catacombs, several mechanics are changed or disabled:
- [[Potions|Potion]] effects from outside the Catacombs are paused while inside.
- Only one [[Potions|potion]] may be consumed or splashed per run, with the exception of [[Stamina Potion|Stamina]], [[Healing Potion|Healing]], and [[Mana Potion|Mana Potions]]. [[God Potion|God Potions]] cannot be consumed.
  - Players can receive effects from multiple potions if they are splashed or consumed simultaneously.
- Players may not access the [[Auction House]], [[Bazaar]], [[The Hex|Hex]], or an [[Anvil]], even while under the effect of a [[Booster Cookie]].
- Players may not warp to private islands.
- Players may not ride on mounts, like the [[Armadillo Pet|Armadillo]] or [[Rock Pet]].
- Every hit on an enemy restores 5 + 1% of the player's maximum [[Mana]]. This applies to melee attacks, bow hits, and the Mage Staff passive of the [[Mage]] class.
  - This does not apply to item abilities, such as Guided Bat from the [[Spirit Sceptre]], or class abilities, such as Explosive Shot.
- All [[Speed]] above 100 [[Speed]] is multiplied by 0.67.
- [[Arrows]] deal reduced knockback and do not respect invincibility frames.
- [[Damage]] against bosses is reduced above certain thresholds, depending on the boss.
- [[Dragon Tracer]] is explicitly disabled, [[Healing]] from [[Vampirism]] is reduced by 10x, and slowness from [[Venomous]] is greatly reduced.
- The [[Purse]] is not displayed, and bonuses from items that scale with coins in the Purse do not work.
- All purchases from [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] or NPCs take coins directly from your [[Bank]].
### Classes
Players can choose one of five [[Classes]]: Berserk, Archer, Mage, Tank, and Healer. Each class serves a different purpose and has their own abilities, class passives, and ghost abilities. They are more effective if only one of each are present in a party, although multiple players can choose the same class if they wish.
- Berserks prioritize dealing as much melee [[Damage]] as possible.
- Archers prioritize dealing as much ranged [[Damage]] with a [[Bows|bow]] as possible.
- Mages prioritize using their item abilities to deal as much magic damage as possible.
  - A sub-sect of Mage, known as Left-Click Mage, uses their Mage Staff passive to deal high amounts of melee [[Damage]].
- Tanks prioritize staying near their teammates and diverting as much [[Damage]] as possible to themselves.
- Healers prioritize providing [[Healing]] and support to their teammates and themselves.
### Ghosts
When a player loses all their [[Health]] in the Catacombs, they do not die traditionally. Instead, they turn into a Ghost and function as a spectator with unique abilities and limited access to the [[SkyBlock Menu]].
Dying does not remove coins held in the [[Purse]] or degrade the [[Piggy Bank]], regardless of whether a [[Booster Cookie]] is active. Ghosts can be revived during the run. If a player leaves a Catacombs run and rejoins it, they can return as a Ghost and be revived.
Ghosts can only interact with the environment through their abilities: these abilities depend on their chosen [[Classes|Class]], and are listed below.
|Class|Name|Ability|
|-|-|-|
|Berserk|Buff Potion|Throws a potion that temporarily gives all teammates in a 10 block radius +30 [[Strength]].; Has a 20s cooldown.|
|Berserk|Ghost Axe|Throws an axe that deals damage based on your Berserk level.; Has a 10s cooldown.|
|Archer|Stun Bow|Temporarily stuns its target upon hit: does not affect bosses.; Has a 10s cooldown.|
|Archer|Healing Bow|Can be used to shoot a teammate, healing them for 20% of their max [[Health]] and granting them +50 [[Strength]] for 10s.; Has a 10s cooldown.|
|Mage|Instant Wall|Spawns a 5x3 wall on the block you are looking at for 10s.; Has a 20s cooldown.|
|Mage|Fireball|Throws an fireball that deals damage based on your Mage level.; Has a 10s cooldown.|
|Tank|Stun Potion|Throws a potion that temporarily stuns all monsters in a 10 block radius.; Has a 20s cooldown.|
|Tank|Absorption Potion|Throws a potion that gives all teammates in a +200 [[Absorption]] for 3s, multiplied by your Tank level * 10.; Has a 20s cooldown.|
|Healer|Revive|Revive yourself after 50s.|
|Healer|Healing Potion|Throws a potion that heals all teammates in a 10 block radius for 10% of their max [[Health]] and +100 [[Health]], multiplied by 100 + your Healer level * 2.; Has a 20s cooldown.|
## Floors
The Catacombs has 7 floors and 1 entrance: beating a floor unlocks the one succeeding it. Defeating [[Necron]], the boss of [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]], unlocks Master Mode, a harder version of the Catacombs will be available.

## Rooms and Exploration
### Generation
A Catacombs floor consists of connected rooms. Special rooms generate in 1x1 spaces, while Standard Rooms can generate in 1x1, 1x2, 1x3, 1x4, 2x2, or L-shaped spaces.
The path to the Blood Room is guarded by a series of Wither Doors and ends at the Blood Door. The Fairy Room always generates along this path. At least 2 Puzzle Rooms generate in a floor.
[[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mobs]] spawn in Standard Rooms at predetermined locations, with some rooms also having predetermined mob types. [[Dungeon Secrets]] also appear in predetermined locations. Finding a Secret grants loot or a team-wide [[Dungeon Blessing|Blessing]] and adds +1 to the [[Dungeon Score|Exploration Score]].
### Room Types
There are several different rooms in the Catacombs, organized by color:
Room: Description
Starter Room: The Starter Room is the room where the Dungeon begins: players can ready up and change their class in this room before the run starts. [[Mort]] can be found here, and he gives players a [[Dungeon Orb]] on lower floors.
Standard Room: Each Dungeon run mostly consists of Standard Rooms. They contain a multitude of mobs and secrets: some mobs will have a yellow star next to their names, marking them as unique. When all unique mobs, or a miniboss, are killed, they will drop an item and mark the room as complete: two of these are guaranteed to be a [[Wither Key]], and one of these is guaranteed to be a [[Blood Key]].
Puzzle Room: The Puzzle Room contains a Puzzle ([[Catacombs Puzzle Rooms]]) that must be completed for the room to be marked as completed. Some can be failed, and others cannot: they can be reset with an [[Architect's First Draft]].
Miniboss Room: The Miniboss Room contains a random Miniboss ([[Minibosses]]): killing them will mark the room as complete. The miniboss can be a [[Angry Archaeologist]], [[Lost Adventurer]], [[King Midas]], [[Shadow Assassin]], or [[Frozen Adventurer]].
Trap Room: The Trap Room consists of several traps and obstacles that must be overcome. They contain several secrets ([[Dungeon Secrets]]) that must be all completed in order to mark the room as complete. Trap Rooms can generate from Floor III ([[The Catacombs - Floor III]]) through Floor VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]), including their Master Mode ([[Catacombs]]) counterparts.
Fairy Room: The Fairy Room consists of a large tree in the middle, with several Fairies ([[Fairy]]) surrounding it that drop a [[Revive Stone]] when killed. A healing pool can be found near the tree. Killing a Fairy here revives a dead player, and a dead player can also kill one to revive themselves.
Blood Room: The Blood Room is the final room that must be completed before the boss room can be accessed. [[The Watcher]] will summon lots of Undead that must be all killed in order for the room to be marked as completed: on higher floors, they will receive modifiers and some will take the appearance of previous bosses, like [[Bonzo]], [[Scarf]], the [[Spirit Bear]], or Sadan's Giants ([[Sadan]]).
### Magical Map
The [[Magical Map]] replaces the [[SkyBlock Menu]] while in the main section of a Catacombs floor and shows the layout of the floor, player locations, and room completion status. Entering the [[Bosses|BOSS]] room restores the normal [[SkyBlock Menu]].
The map uses the following indicators:
- ? on a gray background: the room has not been explored.
- Nothing: the room is uncleared.
- ✘: the room has been failed.
- ✔: the room has been cleared and at least 1 Secret remains undiscovered.
- ✔: the room has been cleared and all of its Secrets have been discovered.
Pressing Right Click or Left Click while holding the map runs /sbmenu.
## Exploration and Rewards
Dungeon loot is obtained from [[Dungeon Secrets|Secrets]], drops from [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mobs]], and [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]].
### Finishing a Run
A Catacombs run ends when one of the following occurs:
- The [[Bosses|BOSS]] is defeated.
- All players in the run have either died and become [[Ghosts]] or left the run.
- 60 minutes pass without defeating the [[Bosses|BOSS]].
Players do not need to complete every room or defeat every mob to complete a run.
### Dungeon Score
Dungeon Score is a value comprised of four categories: Skill, or the number of deaths and [[Dungeon Puzzle Rooms|puzzles]] failed in a run; Exploration, or the percentage of the floor completed and the number of [[Dungeon Secrets|secrets]] completed; Speed, or the time taken to complete a run; and Bonus, which varies depending on the floor and active modifiers, like [[Paul]]'s EZPZ perk.
- 0-99 Score grants a D.
- 100-159 Score grants a C.
- 160-229 Score grants a B.
- 230-269.4 Score grants a A.
- 270-299 Score grants a S.
- ≥300 Score grants a S+.
For more in-depth calculations, see [[Dungeon Score]].
### Dungeon Reward Chests
- When a floor is successfully cleared, player(s) can spend [[Coins]] to unlock different tiers of Dungeon Reward Chests, with each tier increasing in price and quality of loot.
- The total Score achieved at the end of a Dungeon run dictates which [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] are unlocked. Completion of the floor unlocks the Wood, Gold, and Diamond chests; an A unlocks the Emerald chest; an S unlocks the Obsidian chest, and an S+ unlocks the Bedrock chest while on [[The Catacombs - Floor V|Floor V]] or higher. While on [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]], you unlock an additional chest per phase of the boss fight completed.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
- When obtaining an item classified as an RNG Drop, the following dialogue is displayed:
  ----------------------------------- RNG DROP! <username> just found a <item>! ----------------------------------- Items classified as RNG Drops include: [[Livid Dagger]][Confirm] [[Shadow Fury]][Confirm] [[Last Breath]][Confirm] [[Giant's Sword]] [[Fel Skull]][Confirm] [[Precursor Eye]][Confirm] [[Necron's Handle]] [[Necron's Blade Scrolls|Implosion]] [[Necron's Blade Scrolls|Wither Shield]] [[Necron's Blade Scrolls|Shadow Warp]]
## Dungeonizing and Essence
Items used in the Catacombs can be either normal items or Dungeon items. Normal items do not have a DUNGEON tag in their item type, while Dungeon items do. Some normal items can be converted into Dungeon items using [[Essence]], a process commonly called dungeonizing.
Essence is a currency used in the Catacombs that can be spent on upgrades at various NPCs, used to upgrade items, and convert items to Dungeon items.
Dungeon items generally receive increased stats while in the Catacombs. The stat boosts depend on your Catacombs level, the number of [[Star Upgrades]] the item has, and the number of [[Master Star|Master Stars]] applied.
If a [[Slayer]] item is converted into a Dungeon item, it can be used inside the Catacombs without meeting its Slayer level requirement. Most Dungeon items still require a certain Catacombs level. Gear dropped by mobs also uses the entry requirement of the floor it dropped on while inside that Dungeon instance. For example, a [[Zombie Knight Armor|Zombie Knight Chestplate]] dropped from [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]] requires Catacombs XIV (14) normally, but Catacombs XXIV (24) while inside the Catacombs.
Standard [[Star Upgrades]] can be obtained on a variety of items that are not limited to being Catacombs-exclusive; however, [[Master Star|Master Stars]] can only be applied to Dungeon items.
### Stat Bonuses
The stat bonuses from your Catacombs level and Star Upgrades are calculated separately and stack additively on top of the final state increase, only modifying the stats of the item itself.
While in the Catacombs, each Star Upgrade grants a +10% stat boost, adding up to a total stat boost of +50%; each Master Star grants a +5% stat boost on top of the initial increase, totaling to a +75% increase in stats across the board.
[[General's Medallion]] additionally grants a +1% stat boost per digit, up to +5%.
Example: Catacombs 27 [+255%] + 5 stars [+50%] + 2 Master Stars [+10%] + 1000 Secret Counter (4 Digits) [+4%] = +319%, while in Master Mode.
This boost multiplies eligible stats by (100% + 319%)×.
Some [[Stats]] are not boosted by every source, such as [[Attack Speed]], [[Crit Chance]], and [[Ability Damage]]. These stats do not receive the Catacombs Level stat boost, but can receive [[Star Upgrades|Stars]], [[Master Star|Master Stars]], and [[General's Medallion]] digits boosts.
Stats affected on Dungeon items:

### Item Quality
Dungeon items that drop from [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mobs]] and [[Minibosses]] will drop with different base stats depending on the floor it was dropped on and a randomly generated number that dictates the stat increase percentage.
There are up to 10 possible 'tiers' of base stats, dependent on the floor the item was dropped on.
|Tier|Floor "Normal" Mode|Floor Master Mode|
|-|-|-|
|1|[[The Catacombs - Entrance]]||
|1|[[The Catacombs - Floor I]]||
|2|[[The Catacombs - Floor II]]||
|3|[[The Catacombs - Floor III]]||
|4|[[The Catacombs - Floor IV]]|The Catacombs - Floor I (Master Mode) ([[The Catacombs - Floor I]])|
|5|[[The Catacombs - Floor V]]|The Catacombs - Floor II (Master Mode) ([[The Catacombs - Floor II]])|
|6|[[The Catacombs - Floor VI]]|The Catacombs - Floor III (Master Mode) ([[The Catacombs - Floor III]])|
|7|[[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]|The Catacombs - Floor IV (Master Mode)|
|8||The Catacombs - Floor V (Master Mode)|
|9||The Catacombs - Floor VI (Master Mode)|
|10||The Catacombs - Floor VII (Master Mode)|
The bonus stats of dropped items can reach up to a +50% increase, which will result in its [[Rarity]] being upgraded.
## Dungeoneering
[[Dungeoneering]] is a [[Skills|Skill]] that is leveled up through completing runs of the Catacombs. As players complete or fail Dungeon runs, they will increment Catacombs XP and eventually upgrade their Catacombs skill. Each level grants increased [[Health]] and [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]], and grants a multiplicative stat multiplier on Dungeon items of up to +485%.
Additionally, the first 5 completions each day award a 40% bonus to Catacombs XP. Failed runs do not count towards this bonus.
## Trivia
- [[The Professor]] was referred to as Oak on the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]].
  - This was a reference to Professor Oak from the Pokémon franchise. The reason behind the change is unknown.
- The Catacombs was intended to have 10 floors, with [[Maxor]] in Floor 7, [[Goldor]] in Floor 8, [[Storm]] in Floor 9, and [[Necron]] in Floor 10. The other Wither Lords were initially scrapped with the release of [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]], but were re-added with the release of [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|Master Mode - Floor VII]].

|VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]])|Large (Map Size 6x6)|MASTER [[Maxor]]; MASTER [[Storm]]; MASTER [[Goldor]]; MASTER [[Necron]]|The Wither Lords|Disciples of the Wither King. Inherited the Catacombs eons ago. Never defeated, feared by anything living AND dead.||36|

# Class Milestones
Categories: Temp/Using Skill Aliases, Dungeons, Mechanics
Class Milestones are milestones which reflect a player's contribution to the Dungeon run.
## Mechanics
Class milestones are milestones that mark the total damage dealt for [[Berserk]], total damage dealt for [[Mage]], ranged damage dealt for [[Archer]], damage tanked and dealt for [[Tank]], or health healed for [[Healer]]. Each class has 9 milestones, and higher floors have higher requirements for milestones.
Players must reach at least Milestone 2 to open [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Post-Boss Chests]]. The [[Dungeoneering]] XP players receive at the end of the run will be reduced if the player didn't reach Class Milestones 3.
Reaching a milestone causes a message to appear: e.g. Healer Milestone ①: You have healed 500 total damage this run!
## The Catacombs

## Usage
Sword:
Conjuring [[Damage]]: +68 [[Strength]]: +36 [[Intelligence]]: +85
[Effects]
[Reduced the cooldown of Guided Sheep by 5 seconds, and prevents Guided Sheep from spawning randomly while holding this item.]
The Conjuring, being a sword, can be used as a melee weapon: while in the [[Catacombs]], it reduces the cooldown of Guided Sheep by 5s and prevents them from spawning randomly.
### Stat Scaling
The base [[Stats]] of this Item varies based on the floor of [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] it was obtained on. The stats can also be increased by a random amount, up to 50%.

## Trivia
- The Conjuring was implemented upon the release of the [[Catacombs]], but wasn't obtainable up until Update 0.11.2.
- Attempting to use the Guided Sheep ability outside of the [[Catacombs]] displays the following message: This ability can only be used inside dungeons!

## Obtaining

## Usage
Sword:
Dreadlord Sword [[Damage]]: +52 [[Strength]]: +23 [[Intelligence]]: +85
[Effects]
[Ability: Dreadlord RIGHT CLICK; Shoot a skull that deals 500 damage.; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 40; Intelligence Scaling (Damage Calculation): 0.3]
The Dreadlord Sword, being a sword, can be used for melee damage: however, it possesses low [[Damage]] and [[Strength]], and is primarily used for magic damage. Its Dreadlord ability shoots a wither skull in the direction the wielder is facing, exploding on impact for 500 damage to all mobs within 3 blocks. This ability has no cooldown and costs 40 [[Mana]].
### Stats Scaling
The base [[Stats]] of this Item varies based on the floor of [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] it was obtained on. The stats can also be increased by a random amount, up to 50%.

## Trivia
- The Dreadlord Sword's ability cannot be used on the player's [[Private Island]] or [[The Garden]].
- The Dreadlord ability is based on the Dreadlord class in [[References to other Hypixel minigames|Mega Walls]], another Hypixel minigame. However, in Mega Walls, 3 skulls are fired at a time.
- The Dreadlord ability used to be able to destroy dropped items and move armor stands.

# Dungeoneering
Aliases: Catacombs Exp, Catacombs Experience, Catacombs Level, Catacombs Skill, Catacombs XP, Catacombs Xp, Catacombs exp, Catacombs experience, Catacombs level, Catacombs skill, Catacombs xp, Dungeon Xp, Dungeon xp
Categories: Temp/Using Skill Aliases, Dungeons, Potions, Skills
[Dungeoneering]
Dungeoneering is a major [[Skills|Skill]] that revolves around the [[Catacombs]]. It is comprised of [[Catacombs]] levels and Class masteries: the latter consists of individual masteries for each [[Classes|Class]], being [[Healer]], [[Mage]], [[Berserk]], [[Archer]], and [[Tank]].
## Leveling XP
As players complete or fail Dungeon runs, they will increment [[Catacombs]] XP and eventually upgrade their [[Catacombs]] skill. Each level grants increased [[Health]] and [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]], and grants a multiplicative stat multiplier on Dungeon items of up to +485%.
Additionally, the first 5 completions each day award a 40% bonus to [[Catacombs]] XP. Failed runs do not count towards this bonus.
## Classes
In the same manner as players gain [[Catacombs]] XP, they also level up their Classes. Players will gain Class XP depending on their selected Class and the Class their teammates select. Cumulative rewards for leveling up Classes are below.
Show/Hide Dungeoneering Class Rewards
Class: Cumulative rewards at Level XXV (25)
Healer: +25% Renew Healing +0.5% Healing Aura Healing +12% Ghost Healing Aura Healing +50 Ghost Healing Potion Health Restored +2.5% Healing Circle Healing +2.5 Healing Circle Radius +5 Healing Circle Duration +25% Wish Shield Percent -25 Revive Cooldown
Mage: +75 [[Intelligence]] +80,000 Guided Sheep Damage +64,000 Fireball Damage +12% Cooldown Reduction
Berserk: +10% Melee Damage +10% Bloodlust Damage +25% Throwing Axe Damage +40,000 Ghost Axe Damage +640,000 Ragnarok Summon Health +2% Walk Speed +10 Ghost Buff Potion Strength
Archer: +25 Extra Arrow Chance +20% Arrow Damage +50 Bouncy Arrow Chance -10 Explosive Shot Cooldown
Tank: +25 [[Defense]] +5% [[Defense]] +5000 Seismic Wave Damage +250 [[Absorption]] Health
## Dungeons
In Dungeons, the player's Dungeoneering skill affects their dungeon gear multiplier. Each dungeon will likely have a skill of its own. Currently, the only dungeon and corresponding skill that exists is [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]]. In the [[Dungeon Hub]], there is 1 other door, suggesting that there may be 2 dungeons in total. However, the other door is marked as "Not coming soon".
Dungeon skills can be leveled up to level L (50). After level 50, players can gain further, cosmetic levels every 200 million XP. This also applies to class levels.

## Essence
Essence is used to craft new items with [[Malik]], the Blacksmith.
Players can check how much Essence they have by opening the SkyBlock menu, opening the "Your Skills" menu on the left, clicking the Dungeoneering Skill (dead bush), and hovering over the pink/purple cube "Your Essence" at the bottom right. This can also be done by talking with [[Malik]], accessing the Craft menu using the right icon, and hovering over the same pink/purple skull, or by simply holding tab.
Currently, 7 types of Essence can be obtained from Dungeons, and Wither Essence by completing races in the [[Dungeon Hub]].
Supposedly, defeating mobs of each type in a dungeon will give the corresponding type of essence.
The types of [[Essence]] that can be obtained from dungeons are as follows:
- Undead
- Dragon
- Gold
- Diamond
- Wither
- Spider
- Ice
## Trivia
- The [[Health]] from [[Catacombs]] level is only given while in the [[Catacombs]].
- The first player to reach [[Catacombs|Catacombs XL (40)]] and obtain the "True Adventurer" [[Achievements|Achievement]] was 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] egom.
- The first player to reach [[Catacombs|Catacombs L (50)]] was 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] Celatrix (better known as DeathStreeks).
- The first player to reach [[Catacombs|Catacombs C (100)]] was 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] tmoe617.
- The first player to reach 1 billion [[Catacombs]] XP was 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] Celatrix.
- The first player to pass the upper bound of a signed 32-bit integer (231−1) in [[Catacombs]] XP was 6[[Ranks|[MVP++]]] Vortie.
  - This corresponds to [[Catacombs|Catacombs LVII (57)]] (about 89% to [[Catacombs|Catacombs LVIII (58)]]).
- The first player to reach a Class Average of 50 was 6[[Ranks|[MVP++]]] Vortie.
- The highest level reached by any player as of September 2026 is [[Catacombs|Catacombs CLXI (161)]] (Achieved by 6[[Ranks|[MVP++]]] BOBSTEVE21).

# Dungeons
Aliases: Dungeon, Dungeonized, Dungeonizing, Dungeons Master Mode, Dungeons Update, Floor
Categories: Temp/Using Skill Aliases, Dungeons, Catacombs
Dungeons are PvE raid activities in which a team of 1 to 5 players work together to defeat [[Catacombs Mobs|mobs]] within a procedurally generated series of [[Catacombs Rooms|rooms]], ending in a [[Bosses|BOSS]] fight. Players are rewarded based on their performance within the Dungeon with [[Dungeoneering]] XP and items within [[Dungeon Reward Chest|chests]]. The rewards scale up with the difficulty of the Dungeon; the more difficult missions can drop some of the strongest items in the game.
Currently, the only released Dungeons are the [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] and its [[Catacombs|MASTER MODE]] variant. Another two Dungeons have been hinted at, one with a forest theme and another with a desert theme, shown in the [[Dungeon Hub]] and Dungeoneering skill menu as "Not Coming Soon". A [[Kuudra]] section has taken the place of the desert themed section, making the status of the "Not Coming Soon" Dungeons unknown.
Completing a new dungeon floor (including in [[Catacombs|MASTER MODE]]) rewards [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]].
## Overview
Dungeons consist of procedurally generated rooms that players progress through before fighting a [[Bosses|BOSS]]. The only released Dungeon is the [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]], which contains the currently documented Dungeon mechanics. For more information about its rooms, rules, floors, items, scoring, and rewards, see [[Catacombs]].
## Mechanics
### Generation
Dungeon layouts are procedurally generated from connected rooms. The room types, sizes, and generation rules of the currently released Dungeon are documented at [[Catacombs|Catacombs#Rooms and Exploration]].
### Gear Mechanics
Dungeon Items can receive increased stats while inside a Dungeon. The [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] uses [[Essence]], [[Star Upgrades]], [[Master Star|Master Stars]], and [[Catacombs]] levels for these bonuses. See [[Catacombs|Catacombs#Dungeonizing and Essence]] for details.
### Rules
Dungeon runs can alter or disable normal SkyBlock mechanics. The rules currently used in the [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] are documented at [[Catacombs|Catacombs#Rules]].
### Floors
Dungeons are divided into Floors with their own requirements, difficulty, bosses, and rewards. See [[Catacombs|Catacombs#Floors]] for the Floors of the currently released Dungeon.
### Classes
Players can choose one of five [[Classes]] in Dungeons: [[Healer]], [[Berserk]], [[Mage]], [[Archer]], or [[Tank]]. Each class provides unique buffs and abilities that improve as the class is leveled.
### Loot
Dungeon loot can be obtained from [[Dungeon Secrets|Secrets]], mob drops, and [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]]. See [[Catacombs|Catacombs#Exploration and Rewards]] for the loot and reward systems of the [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]].
### Finishing, Scoring and Rewards
Dungeon completion and reward systems can vary by Dungeon. The [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] uses [[Dungeon Score]] to determine the [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] available after a successful run. See [[Catacombs|Catacombs#Exploration and Rewards]] for details.
## Trivia
- The [[Dungeoneering]] skill is most likely a reference to the skill of the same name in RuneScape, where it is mainly leveled up in Daemonheim, where players complete randomly generated dungeons. Likewise, Dungeons uses similar mechanics.

|Jul 15, 2020|Arrows now bypass invulnerability frames in Dungeons.|
|Jul 15, 2020|Archer Rapid Fire arrows now have Aiming 1|
|Jul 15, 2020|Journal pages and Dungeon NPCs no longer count as secrets|
|Jul 16, 2020|Added a queue system for entering Dungeons when servers are full.|
|Jul 17, 2020|The Dungeon Map now shows 2 kinds of check marks: a white checkmark for when all required mobs have been slain, and a green checkmark for when all secrets have been found in a certain room.|
|Jul 17, 2020|Added a secret counter to the action bar|
|Jul 17, 2020|Containers now can only be opened once. After they are opened, they will remain opened instead of disappearing and their contents will drop on the ground.|
|Jul 30, 2020|Added Catacombs Floor 4.|
|Jul 30, 2020|Added Ice Essence, which drops from Frozen Adventurers.|
|Jul 30, 2020|Changed mana regen in Dungeons. Players regenerate 90% less mana, but any hit on enemy mobs grants 1% + 5 mana.|
|Jul 30, 2020|Improved aggro system.|
|Jul 30, 2020|Fixed Berserk melee damage buff and Archer melee damage nerf applying to all damage types.|
|Jul 30, 2020|Unstable Lost Adventurers now deal lightning damage; Holy ones heal faster.|
|Jul 30, 2020|Mana regen ticks are now rounded up.|
|Jul 30, 2020|Non-mage mana regen debuff decreased to 75%.|
|Jul 30, 2020|Buffed passive mana regen for all classes: Mage to 20%, Tank and Berserk to 35%, Healer and Archer to 50%.|
|Jul 30, 2020|Added Frozen Adventurers to regular rooms, fixed an issue that was making them too tanky and gave them 1 extra Ice essence drop.|
|Aug 1, 2020|Mort won't give Dungeon Orbs anymore in floor 3 and up if the player does not have one already.|
|Aug 7, 2020|Added the Essence Shop, where players can buy perks for large amounts of Undead and Wither essence.|
|Aug 7, 2020|Players can now enter Catacombs level requirements not higher than their own when creating a party.|
|Aug 7, 2020|Mana regen for all classes reverted to 100%.|
|Aug 7, 2020|Mana on hit no longer applies to the mage staff.|
|Aug 7, 2020|Added magic damage resistance to Dungeon mobs: 10% for most melee, 30% for ranged, 50-80% for magic-type. Certain bosses and mini-bosses have this resistance. Chaos Guardian magic damage resistance reduced from 90% to 80%.|
|Aug 7, 2020|Archer's Explosive Shot now fires 3 arrows in an arc, and can blow up anything Superboom TNT can.|
|Aug 7, 2020|Archer now has an extra passive: Arrow Bounce Chance|
|Aug 7, 2020|Archer Bone Plating cooldown reduced by 5s|
|Aug 7, 2020|Healing Circle is now 1 block wider|
|Aug 7, 2020|Healers can sneak to activate their healing circle beneath them.|
|Aug 7, 2020|Activating the Healer ultimate when a teammate is in the red reduces the cooldown of the Wish by 10s per red health bars.|
|Aug 7, 2020|Berserk's Bloodlust used to increased the Base damage of their sword, it'll now apply post multipliers (essentially a large buff to bloodlust).|
|Aug 7, 2020|The damage scaling per level on every class ability is now 5 times higher. If a class ability would gain 1000 damage on level up, it now gains 5000 damage.|
|Aug 7, 2020|Entering the boss room now fully replenishes the mana pool and resets class ability cooldowns.|
|Sep 17, 2020|0.9.1|Leaving a dungeon without killing the boss will now reduce the score by 30%|
|Sep 17, 2020|0.9.1|Added stats to Golden and Diamond boss heads. On their respective floor, their stats are doubled.|
|Sep 17, 2020|0.9.1|Dungeons stats are now added to the API|

|Sep 17, 2020|0.9.1|Catacombs Floors 2, 3 and 4 are now approximately 5% easier|

|Sep 17, 2020|0.9.1|Starting Floor 5, obtaining an S+ rank will unlock the Bedrock chest, that can contain extremely strong items.|
|Sep 17, 2020|0.9.1|Killing mobs in the dungeons will now drop their loot client side. Whenever a mob drops loot, the system now randomly distribute the item among eligible players. The killer, and all players within 20 blocks of the monster are all eligible for the roll. Nearby players have a weight of 1, and the killer has a weight of 5 to be selected.|

## Usage
Sword:
Earthen Blade [[Defense]]: +73 [[Damage]]: +105
[Effects]
[This shard automatically adapts to its user inside Dungeons, allowing you to perform your class ability. Reduces the cooldown of Seismic Wave by 2s.]
The Earthen Blade, being a sword, can be used as a melee weapon. It has the unique property of reducing the cooldown of Seismic Wave by 2s: additionally, the wielder can right-click while holding an Earthen Blade to use their class ability.

# Equipment
Aliases: Farming Equipment, Farming equipment, Fishing Equipment, Fishing equipment, Foraging Equipment, Foraging equipment, Mining Equipment, Mining equipment
Categories: Temp/Using Skill Aliases, Equipment
[Equipment]
Category: [[Armor & Equipment]]
Equipment is a category of equipable items that increases a [[Player|player's]] [[Stats]] when put in the appropriate Equipment slot. Equipment comes in 4 categories: [[Necklaces]], [[Cloaks]], [[Belts]] and [[Gloves]]/[[Bracelets]].
## Overview
- In SkyBlock, "Equipment" is a term that refers to either equipment piece(s) or equipment set(s) or the concept of Equipment.
- An "Equipment Piece" refers to either a [[Necklaces]], [[Cloaks]], [[Belts]] and [[Gloves]]/[[Bracelets]].
  - At least half a dozen of synonyms are used to refer to each type of Equipment Piece but they all end up being equipped in the same 4 slots.
- An "Equipment Set" refers to a combination of Equipment pieces, often sharing (similar) [[Stats]], source, purpose and may even hold a Set Bonus. This may also include [[Armor]] pieces.
### Bonus
- A "Bonus" is a buff that is granted if the player wears equipment pieces bearing that same Bonus. This may also include [[Armor]] pieces.
  - A "Piece Bonus" is a buff that is granted if the player wears the equipment piece.
    - An "Ability" is a synonym for "Piece Bonus".
  - A "Full Set" Bonus will only be granted if the player wears ALL of the equipment pieces belonging to that equipment set.
  - A "Tiered" Bonus will be granted if the player wears part(s) of the equipment pieces belonging to that equipment set.
    - The Bonus may have a minimum amount of equipment pieces to be active.
    - The Bonus typically becomes more powerful as the player wears more equipment pieces of that equipment set.
- If the conditions are NOT fulfilled for the Bonus to be active, then the Bonus name in the item lore will be greyed out.
- If the conditions are fulfilled for the Bonus to be active, then the Bonus name in the item lore will be golden.
- Some Equipment Pieces do not belong to any Equipment Set.
- The majority of Equipments do not have any Bonus.
### Controls
- An Equipment Piece may be equipped:
  - by Right-Clicking while holding it.
    - This will automatically place it in its appropriate slot.
    - This does not work if the appropriate slot is already occupied.
  - Left-Clicking, Right-Clicking or Drop Key it in the inventory while the /stats menu opened.
    - This will automatically place it in its appropriate slot.
    - If the appropriate slot is alreayd busy, this will replace it and the previous Equipment piece will be returned in the inventory.
- An Equipment Piece may be un-equipped:
  - by clicking it in the /stats menu.
- The [[Equipment Wardrobe]] can be used to store and freely swap between Equipment Sets in a click.
- The [[Loadouts]] can be used to freely swap between Equipment Sets, Armor Sets, Power Stones, Tuning Points in a click.
## Upgrades
These upgrades can be applied to any Equipment piece, increasing its [[Stats]] and boosts.
|Example|Image|Upgrade|Description|
|-|-|-|-|
|Blooming Mangrove Necklace|[[Anvil]]|Reforges ([[Reforging]])|Reforges ([[Reforging]]) range in effects, give stats, and sometimes an extra ability. The [[Stats]] of the Reforge ([[Reforging]]) depends on the [[Rarity]] of the item and cost more [[Coins]] to apply at higher rarities.|
|Mangrove Necklace|[[Enchanting]]|[[Enchantments]]|[[Enchantments]] grant a variety of effects, stat boosts, and sometimes extra abilities. They can be applied to Equipment in an [[Anvil]] and cost [[Experience]] to combine.|
|Mangrove Necklace|[[Enchanting]]|[[Ultimate Enchantments]]|[[Ultimate Enchantments]] are better than ordinary enchantments and give a unique, and usually powerful, bonus. However, only one Ultimate Enchantment can be applied per piece.|
|Mangrove Necklace|[[Recombobulator 3000]]|[[Recombobulator 3000]]|A [[Recombobulator 3000]] upgrade the rarity of an armor piece by 1, thus amplifying its reforge power. They can only be used once per piece.|
|Mangrove Necklace|[[Common Foraging Fortune Booster]]|[[Boosters]]|[[Boosters]] can be applied to Foraging equipment, tools and armor, to grant various bonuses, depending on the specific booster used. All boosters can be applied to the same item, but only one each. The stats do NOT scale with rarity of the item.|
|Pendant of Divan|[[Gemstone]]|[[Gemstone]]|Gemstones ([[Gemstone]]) add various stats to items, which varies with the type of gemstone. They can only be applied to pieces with a corresponding [[Gemstone Slot]]. Their power ranges depending on the tier of Gemstone and the rarity of the item.|
|Pendant of Divan|[[Divan's Powder Coating]]|[[Divan's Powder Coating]]|[[Divan's Powder Coating]] can be applied exclusively to Divan Armor ([[Armor of Divan]]), any Drill ([[Drills]]), and [[Pendant of Divan]]. When applied to Equipment, it grants 10 [[Mining Fortune]].|
|Balloon Snake ✪✪✪✪✪|[[Wither Essence]]|[[Essence]]|[[Essence]] can be used to dungeonize and star Equipment. This grants an extra stat boost inside and out of a Dungeon ([[Dungeons]]).|
|Balloon Snake ✪✪✪✪✪➊|[[Master Star]]|[[Master Star]]|Master Stars ([[Master Star]]) can be used to further dungeonize an Equipment piece. This goes up to an additional 5 stars, and gives a larger stat boost when inside Master Mode ([[Catacombs]]) Dungeons.|

|[[Vanquished Glowstone Gauntlet]]|From Glowstone Dust VII (7) ([[Glowstone Dust]]),|has a Combat XXII (22) ([[Combat]]) requirement.|
|[[Ancient Cloak]]|From Red Sand V (5) ([[Red Sand]]),|has a Combat XXII (22) ([[Combat]]) requirement.|
|[[Dwarven Handwarmers]]|From [[The Forge]] for Flawless Gemstones ([[Gemstone]]), a Tungsten ([[Tungsten Plate]]) and an Umber Plates ([[Umber Plate]]) and 4 hours,|has a Heart of the Mountain Tier 8 ([[Heart of the Mountain]]) requirement.|
|[[Pendant of Divan]]|From [[The Forge]] for a [[Shattered Locket]], a [[Perfect Plate]], Divan Fragments ([[Divan Fragment]]) and 7 days,|has a Heart of the Mountain Tier 10 ([[Heart of the Mountain]]) requirement.|
Equipment outside of these [[Skills]] are willingly omitted for keeping this section readable. The section below provides the full list.

# Healing
Categories: Temp/Using Skill Aliases, Player Statistics, Mechanics
[Healing]
Healing is the act of regenerating [[Health]]. There are 3 stats that determine healing: [[Health Regen]] increases the amount of [[Health]] passively regenerated, [[Vitality]] serves as a pool (similar to [[Mana]]) that healing abilities draw from, and [[Mending]] increases the amount of healing the player gives to other players.
## Overview
Healing from an ability is calculated in this order:
1. The ability's own base healing value, for example 320 [[Health]] for the [[Zombie Sword]].
2. Multiply by the [[Book of Progression]]'s Stacked Healing bonus, if that modifier is selected. The bonus is +0.01% per [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock Level]], capped at +5% at [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock Level 500]].
3. When the healing comes from another player, multiply by Mending100.
[[Vitality]] is not a multiplier on the amount healed. It is the cost pool that healing abilities spend from and regenerates at 5% of maximum per second. Spending Vitality never changes the size of the heal.
Natural regeneration is a separate system and does not use Mending or Vitality: (MaxHealth100+1.5)×HealthRegen100
The player regenerates [[Health]] naturally over time at a base interval of 1s. At the default 100 [[Health Regen]], this is 1% of max Health plus 1.5 Health every second.
## Increasing Healing
There are a multitude of items that allow the player to heal. These usually fall into several categories:
- [[Wands|Healing Wands]] are unlocked from [[Zombie Slayer]] and heal the player for a set amount of [[Health]] per second, over a duration of 5s to 6s, at a 25 [[Vitality]] cost. The listed values are base healing.
- The [[Zombie Sword]] and its upgrades, the [[Ornate Zombie Sword|Ornate]] and [[Florid Zombie Sword]], all possess the Instant Heal ability, which heals the player for a flat amount of [[Health]] plus nearby players, for 25 [[Vitality]] and no charges. The listed values are base healing.
- [[Power Orbs]] are a form of [[Deployables|Deployable]] unlocked from [[Wolf Slayer]]. When placed, they heal the player for a flat amount every second, heal other players for half that amount, and grant [[Health Regen]], alongside other buffs.
  - [[Flares]] are a similar form of [[Deployables|Deployable]], unlocked from [[Blaze Slayer]], which grant [[Vitality]] alongside other buffs and heal on the [[Crimson Isle]] and [[Kuudra's Hollow]] only.
- Several other items allow the player to heal.
  - The [[Gloomlock Grimoire]] converts [[Soulflow (Mechanic)|Soulflow]] to [[Mana|Overflow Mana]] at the cost of [[Health]], and can spend [[Vitality]] to heal a large flat amount.
  - Several [[Potions]], such as [[Healing Potion|Healing Potions]] and [[Stamina Potion|Stamina Potions]], grant [[Health]] on consumption.
  - A multitude of [[Pets]], [[Armor]], and [[Weapons]] grant healing in some shape and form.

## Tips
- Heavy Armor can be used for it's high [[Defense]] stat and low [[Catacombs]] requirement on earlier floors, but is not recommended to use later due to lack of offensive stats.
## Trivia
- Reforging Heavy Armor to [[Reforging|Heavy]] applies the Extremely Heavy prefix; similarly, reforging it to [[Reforging|Light]] applies the Not So Heavy prefix.
- The Heavy Helmet and Boots are one of 6 armor pieces which have the hex color code of ⁠ #FFFFFF. The others are [[Armor of the Pack|Helmet of the Pack]], [[Kelly's T-Shirt]], [[Leggings of the Coven]] (white variant) and [[Snow Suit Armor]].

The Ice Spray Wand is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] [[Swords|Sword]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv189-850]|[[Frozen Adventurer]]|1|8Legendary Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.1%|
## Usage
Sword:
Ice Spray Wand [[Damage]]: +65 [[Intelligence]]: +152
[Effects]
[Ability: Ice Spray RIGHT CLICK; Produces a cone of ice in front of the caster that deals 17,000 damage to mobs and freezes them in place for 5 seconds! Frozen mobs take 10% increased damage!; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 50; Cooldown: 5s; Intelligence Scaling (Damage Calculation): 0.1]
The Ice Spray Wand's Ice Spray ability creates a cone-shaped radius 7 blocks long and 60º wide: this cone can pass through walls. All mobs within the radius take damage, are frozen in place for 5s, and take 10% more damage from all sources. Mobs that are 'frozen' can still take knockback and use abilities. This ability costs 50 [[Mana]] and has a cooldown of 5s.
Mobs immune to the [[Aspect of the Dragons]]' Dragon's Rage ability are immune to the Ice Spray ability.

Upgrading the Ice Spray Wand grants the following:
- +8 [[Damage]]
- +17 [[Intelligence]]
- Ice Spray gains +2000 [[Damage]]

|-|-|-|

## Tips
- While it is ill-advised to use the Ice Spray Wand as a primary weapon due to its low base [[Damage]] and long cooldown, it is incredibly useful for clearing due to its ability.
## Trivia
- The Ice Spray ability conflicts with other abilities that slow mobs, such as the [[Frozen Scythe]]'s Ice Bolt ability.
