# Milestones
Categories: Temp/Using Skill Aliases, Pets, 0.7.8
Milestones are goals that rewards the [[Player]] items or gifts, when they achieve the goal.
## Pet Milestones
Pet Milestones are milestones that reward the player [[Pets]] when they achieve them. [[Dolphin Pet]]: It is obtained by killing [[Sea Creatures]].
The milestones are as follows:
|Rarity|Sea Creature Kills|
|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|250|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|1000|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|2500|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|5000|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|10000|
[[Rock Pet]]: It is obtained by mining [[Ores]]. [[Netherrack]] and [[End Stone]] are not ores, and do not count.
The milestones are as follows:
|Rarity|Ores Mined|
|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|2500|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|7500|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|20000|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|100000|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|250000|
## Dungeon Milestones
Dungeon Milestones are class goals that give the player the possibility to gain better loot and to earn more class XP after clearing a [[Dungeons|Dungeon]].
The Milestones vary for each class and are greater on higher floors.
Achieving Milestone 2 is required to open the [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Post-Boss Dungeon Chest]], you'll also get reduced [[Catacombs]] and Class Experience if you fail to reach Milestone 3 before the dungeon run ends.
|Class|Actions|
|-|-|
|Archer|Deal Ranged Damage|
|Tank|Deal or Tank Damage|
|Healer|Heal Teammates & damage|
|Berserk|Deal [[Damage]]|
|Mage|Deal [[Ability Damage]]|
## Bestiary Milestone
The [[Bestiary|Bestiary Milestone]] allows players to obtain rewards based on the amount of kills they've accumulated on [[Mob|Mobs]] in [[Locations|different areas]]. The progress of this milestone does not increase with the same Mob types in those different areas. (For instance, an [[Enderman]] on your [[Private Island]] will not count towards your [[Enderman]] collection in [[The End]].)
[[Bestiary]] Milestones are also stages where a player has to level up 10 families. The player will be rewarded with +2 [[Health]] at each milestone, and if the milestone is an even number, the player will also be rewarded [[Combat|Combat 1,000,000]] XP, as long as the milestone is over level 18.
## Other Milestones
The [[Expertise]] Enchantment uses the milestone system to upgrade.
It is upgraded by killing [[Sea Creatures]] while having the Expertise enchantment on your rod.
The milestones are as follows;
|Level|Sea Creatures Killed|
|-|-|
|Ⅰ|N/A|
|Ⅱ|50|
|Ⅲ|100|
|Ⅳ|250|
|Ⅴ|500|
|Ⅵ|1000|
|Ⅶ|2500|
|Ⅷ|5500|
|Ⅸ|10000|
|Ⅹ|15000|
## Trivia
- While players cannot see their Milestones progress, their progress can be retrieved from [[SkyBlock API]].
  - Fishing rods that have been enchanted with [[Expertise]] store expertise_kills in their NBT data.
- You can easily look your progress up on sky.shiiyu.moe, which is a stats tracker.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|May 5, 2020|0.7.8|Added Milestones.|
|Jul 7, 2020|0.8|Added Dungeon Milestones.|
|Sep 8, 2020|0.9|Added [[Expertise]] Enchantment Milestones.|
|Mar 9, 2021|0.11.2|Added Bestiary Milestones.|
Linked from: [[Changelog/2020/July 7]], [[Chapters]], [[Chapters/Torrhus Canyon]], [[Torrhus Canyon]]

### Mob Drops

## Usage
Sword:
Silent Death [[Damage]]: +100 [[Strength]]: +37 [[Crit Chance]]: +1 [[Crit Damage]]: +11
[Effects]
[Ability: Shadowstep RIGHT CLICK; Teleport behind the enemy you are looking at, gaining +25 Strength for 10 seconds. Max range of 20 blocks. Cooldown resets on kills.; Cooldown: 60s]
The Silent Death, being a sword, can be used as a melee weapon. Its Shadowstep ability teleports the wielder behind the enemy they are looking at, up to 20 block away: upon teleporting, the wielder gain +25 [[Strength]] for 10s. This ability has a 60s cooldown, which is reset upon killing an enemy with any weapon: it has no [[Mana]] cost.

## Trivia
- The Shadowstep ability shares a functionality with the Enderman class's skill and the name of the Assassin class's passive, both from [[References to other Hypixel minigames|Mega Walls]].
  - Additionally, it also references the Subtlety Rogue's ability from World of Warcraft: however, the Subtlety Rogue's ability grants increased speed instead of [[Strength]].
- The ability, "Shadowstep", refers to World of Warcraft Subtlety Rogue ability, which does nearly the same thing except it gives speed instead of strength.
- The Silent Death bears a resemblance to the [[Shadow Fury]]: both allow the player to teleport behind enemies to attack them, and both are related to [[Shadow Assassin|Shadow Assassins]].

## Tips
- A [[Skeleton Master Armor|Skeleton Master Chestplate]] can substitute a [[Necron's Armor|Necron's Chestplate]] due to similar stats and its arrow-saving perk when playing as an non [[Mage]] class in the [[Catacombs]], especially when its stats are increased by +50% due to this increasing its rarity from [[Rarity|EPIC]] to [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], allowing one to recombobulate it to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]].
- While wearing [[Skeleton Master Armor|Skeleton Master Chestplate]] and having [[Flint Arrow]] in players' inventory, you can not shoot a long bow for some reason.

[Effects]
[Each piece grants a 1 second cooldown reduction on bone plating! Full Set Bonus: Skeletor; Grants 1 Strength and 1 Crit Chance for every 10 Skeletor kills]
Skeletor Armor's Skeletor full set bonus grants +1 [[Strength]] and +1 [[Crit Damage]] per 10 [[Skeletor]] kills the user has, which caps at 500 kills. Resulting in a maximum of +50 [[Strength]] and +50 [[Crit Damage]]. Additionally, while playing as an [[Archer]] in the [[Catacombs]], the cooldown on Bone Plating is reduced by 1s per piece of Skeletor Armor worn.
